<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子理论中，克隆非正交态的不可能性是基本原理。现代量子学习理论常考虑结构化态类，利用结构开发优于通用态层析的学习算法。这引发问题：对于此类结构化态类，学习与克隆的样本复杂度关系如何？

Method: 使用最近提出的阿贝尔态隐藏子群框架中的表示论工具，以及新引入的结构化随机纯化信道，将稳定子态克隆与经典学习理论中最近引入的概率分布样本放大问题变体联系起来。通过证明具有底层线性结构的分布类的样本放大下界，获得克隆下界。

Result: 对于n量子比特稳定子态，最优克隆样本复杂度为Θ(n)，表明克隆与学习同样困难。这为无克隆定理提供了更细粒度的视角。

Conclusion: 即使对于稳定子态这类结构化态类，克隆与学习同样困难。该研究建立了从基础理论到量子学习理论和量子密码学的连接，为无克隆定理提供了更精细的理解。

Abstract: The impossibility of simultaneously cloning non-orthogonal states lies at the foundations of quantum theory. Even when allowing for approximation errors, cloning an arbitrary unknown pure state requires as many initial copies as needed to fully learn the state. Rather than arbitrary unknown states, modern quantum learning theory often considers structured classes of states and exploits such structure to develop learning algorithms that outperform general-state tomography. This raises the question: How do the sample complexities of learning and cloning relate for such structured classes? We answer this question for an important class of states. Namely, for $n$-qubit stabilizer states, we show that the optimal sample complexity of cloning is $Θ(n)$. Thus, also for this structured class of states, cloning is as hard as learning. To prove these results, we use representation-theoretic tools in the recently proposed Abelian State Hidden Subgroup framework and a new structured version of the recently introduced random purification channel to relate stabilizer state cloning to a variant of the sample amplification problem for probability distributions that was recently introduced in classical learning theory. This allows us to obtain our cloning lower bounds by proving new sample amplification lower bounds for classes of distributions with an underlying linear structure. Our results provide a more fine-grained perspective on No-Cloning theorems, opening up connections from foundations to quantum learning theory and quantum cryptography.

</details>


### [70] [Generation of Schrödinger cat-like states via degenerate dual pump spontaneous four-wave mixing in a $χ^{(3)}$ microring resonator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15275)
*Ranjit Singh,Alexander E. Teretenkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文理论研究了在χ³微环谐振腔中通过简并双泵浦自发四波混频产生非高斯量子态（特别是薛定谔猫态）的方法，通过精确解耦SPM和XPM项，将非线性哈密顿量简化为有效三模相互作用，并采用全量子力学方法分析量子泵浦耗尽效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于开发在微环谐振腔中产生和控制非高斯量子态（特别是薛定谔猫态）的理论框架，超越传统的半经典或参量近似方法，考虑量子泵浦耗尽效应，为量子信息处理和量子计算提供新的平台。

Method: 引入酉变换精确解耦自相位调制和交叉相位调制项，将完整非线性哈密顿量简化为有效三模相互作用；采用林德布拉德主方程处理腔损耗；计算维格纳函数、光子数分布、正交方差、法诺因子、施密特数和保真度等量子态表征量。

Result: 在无耗散情况下，信号模式呈现清晰的非高斯特征，具有结构化的维格纳函数和偶数主导的光子数分布；在耗散情况下（γ_j=0.2），干涉条纹变弱但保真度仍高于0.9，表明鲁棒性；泵浦模式保持高斯特性，两模式均显示超泊松统计和纠缠。

Conclusion: 简并双泵浦自发四波混频在微环谐振腔中是产生和控制猫态的有前景平台，即使在耗散条件下也能保持高保真度，为量子信息应用提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate the generation of non-Gaussian quantum states, specifically Schrödinger cat-like states (SCLSs), via degenerate dual-pump spontaneous four-wave mixing in a $χ^{(3)}$-based microring resonator. By introducing a unitary transformation that exactly decouples the self-phase modulation (SPM) and cross-phase modulation (XPM) terms, we reduce the full nonlinear Hamiltonian to an effective three-mode interaction. The resulting dynamics (decoupled and full Hamiltonians) are studied using the Lindblad master equation, accounting for cavity losses. Unlike semiclassical or parametric approximations, our full quantum mechanical approach explicitly includes quantum pump depletion, which enables the emergence and observation of non-Gaussian features. We compute the Wigner function, photon number distributions, quadrature variances, Fano factor, Schmidt number, and fidelity to characterize the generated states. For the non-dissipative case, we find that the signal mode $\hat{b}_3$ or $\hat{a}_3$ exhibits clear non-Gaussian features with a structured Wigner function and even-dominated photon number distribution, characteristic of an even coherent state. In the presence of dissipation ($γ_j = 0.2$), the interference fringes become faint, odd photon numbers appear, and the fidelity with the ideal state remains high ($>0.9$), indicating robustness. The pump mode $\hat{b}_1$ or $\hat{a}_1$ remains Gaussian, while both modes display super-Poissonian statistics and entanglement ($>2$). Our results demonstrate that degenerate dual-pump spontaneous four-wave mixing in microring resonators is a promising platform for generating and controlling cat-like states under dissipative conditions.

</details>


### [71] [Super-Constant Weight Dicke States in Constant Depth Without Fanout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15298)
*Lucas Gretta,Meghal Gupta,Malvika Raj Joshi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了在QAC⁰电路中制备任意权重Dicke态和对称态的常数深度量子电路构造方法，并建立了FANOUT门资源与Dicke态可制备性之间的紧致对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dicke态作为NISQ时代重要的纠缠资源，在解码量子干涉仪等应用中具有核心作用。然而，现有常数深度电路构造需要FANOUT_n门，而QAC⁰电路仅能实现FANOUT_k（k≤polylog(n)）。因此需要开发更高效的常数深度构造方法。

Method: 使用多量子比特Toffoli门和单量子比特幺正操作，构建了制备权重k≤polylog(n)的n量子比特Dicke态的常数深度电路。通过FANOUT_min(k,n-k)门而非FANOUT_n门实现任意权重Dicke态，并将技术扩展到任意对称态的制备。

Result: 1. 首次在QAC⁰中实现了超常数权重Dicke态的构造；2. 建立了紧致对应关系：当k≤n/2时，权重k的Dicke态可在QAC⁰中制备当且仅当FANOUT_k∈QAC⁰；3. 首次获得了任意对称态的O(1)深度幺正构造。

Conclusion: 该工作为Dicke态和对称态的常数深度制备提供了完整的理论框架，特别适用于支持FANOUT_n门的量子硬件架构（如具有全局纠缠操作的囚禁离子系统），为NISQ时代的量子应用提供了重要工具。

Abstract: An $n$-qubit Dicke state of weight $k$, is the uniform superposition over all $n$-bit strings of Hamming weight $k$. Dicke states are an entanglement resource with important practical applications in the NISQ era and, for instance, play a central role in Decoded Quantum Interferometry (DQI). Furthermore, any symmetric state can be expressed as a superposition of Dicke states.
  First, we give explicit constant-depth circuits that prepare $n$-qubit Dicke states for all $k \leq \text{polylog}(n)$, using only multi-qubit Toffoli gates and single-qubit unitaries. This gives the first $\text{QAC}^0$ construction of super-constant weight Dicke states. Previous constant-depth constructions for any super-constant $k$ required the FANOUT$_n$ gate, while $\text{QAC}^0$ is only known to implement FANOUT$_k$ for $k$ up to $\text{polylog}(n)$. Moreover, we show that any weight-$k$ Dicke state can be constructed with access to FANOUT$_{\min(k,n-k)}$, rather than FANOUT$_n$. Combined with recent hardness results, this yields a tight characterization: for $k \leq n/2$, weight-$k$ Dicke states can be prepared in $\text{QAC}^0$ if and only if FANOUT$_k \in \text{QAC}^0$.
  We further extend our techniques to show that, in fact, \emph{any} superposition of $n$-qubit Dicke states of weight at most $k$ can be prepared in $\text{QAC}^0$ with access to FANOUT$_k$. Taking $k = n$, we obtain the first $O(1)$-depth unitary construction for arbitrary symmetric states. In particular, any symmetric state can be prepared in constant depth on quantum hardware architectures that support FANOUT$_n$, such as trapped ions with native global entangling operations.

</details>

*Kenta Kasai*

TL;DR: 该论文研究了由仿射置换矩阵构建的CSS量子LDPC码族的最小距离的认证上界，通过构造低权重非稳定子逻辑代表元来获得有效上界。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是改进对APM-LDPC量子码距离的上界估计，避免尝试证明一般下界，而是通过构造具体的低权重逻辑代表元来获得更精确的上界。

Method: 开发了统一的框架，利用潜在行关系、受限提升子空间（包括块压缩、选择纤维和CRT条纹构造）、环-8基本陷阱集结构以及解码器失败残差来构造见证元。仅使用搜索生成候选，通过显式的核和行空间排除测试来认证上界。

Result: 将方法应用于代表性APM-LDPC码，改进了先前报告的上界，并在探索的参数范围内提供了具体的认证值。

Conclusion: 该研究为量子LDPC码的距离上界认证提供了系统方法，特别针对具有围长8的APM构造码，通过构造和验证低权重逻辑代表元获得了更精确的上界估计。

Abstract: This paper investigates certified upper bounds on the minimum distance of an explicit family of Calderbank-Shor-Steane quantum LDPC codes constructed from affine permutation matrices. All codes considered here have active Tanner graphs of girth eight. Rather than attempting to prove a general lower bound for the full code distance, we focus on constructing low-weight non-stabilizer logical representatives, which yield valid upper bounds once they are verified to lie in the opposite parity-check kernel and outside the stabilizer row space. We develop a unified framework for such witnesses arising from latent row relations, restricted-lift subspaces including block-compressed, selected-fiber, and CRT-stripe constructions, cycle- 8 elementary trapping-set structures, and decoder-failure residuals. In every case, search is used only to generate candidates; the reported bounds begin only after explicit kernel and row-space exclusion tests have been passed. For the latent part, we also identify a block-compression criterion under which the certification becomes exact. Applying these methods to representative APM-LDPC codes sharpens previously reported upper bounds and provides concrete certified values across the explored parameter range.

</details>

### [73] [Configuration-dependent electronic and optical properties of 2D Mo$_{1-x}$W$_x$S$_2$ alloys across the full composition range](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14377)
*M. Szyszko,M. Birowska*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究通过DFT和蒙特卡洛模拟分析了Mo$_x$W$_{1-x}$S$_2$合金的原子构型对电子和光学性质的影响，发现局部原子排列显著影响能带分裂、谷结构、有效质量和光学选择定则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Mo$_x$W$_{1-x}$S$_2$合金中不同原子构型对材料性质的影响，特别是探索微观原子排列如何影响电子结构和光学性质，而不仅仅是整体成分的作用。

Method: 使用密度泛函理论(DFT)和蒙特卡洛模拟，在整个成分范围内分析多个对称不等价的原子构型，研究其结构稳定性、电子结构和光学性质。

Result: 发现即使没有自旋轨道耦合，整个成分范围内K点处能带边缘都会发生分裂，其中价带顶相对稳定，而导带底显示出强烈的构型依赖性分裂（几meV到几百meV）。特定构型支持超出传统A和B激子的额外光学活性跃迁，而近简并构型则表现出减少的允许跃迁数。空穴有效质量在价带顶显示出构型依赖的各向异性。

Conclusion: 光学活性跃迁的数量和特性不仅由成分决定，还受微观原子排列的调控。局部对称性破缺导致方向依赖的输运特性，表明原子构型在合金性质设计中具有关键作用。

Abstract: Here we analyze multiple symmetry-inequivalent atomic configurations across the entire composition range of the isovalent and isostructural Mo$_x$W$_{1-x}$S$_2$ alloy using density-functional theory and Monte Carlo simulations. Our results show that although structural stability and energetics are largely composition-driven, the electronic and optical properties exhibit configuration dependence, with local atomic arrangements critically shaping band-edge splitting, valley structure, effective-mass anisotropy, and optical selection rules. In contrast to the pristine monolayers, even in the absence of spin-orbit coupling (SOC), splitting of the band edges at the $K$ point is observed across the entire composition range. In particular, while the valence-band maximum (VBM) remains largely robust, the conduction-band minimum (CBM) shows strong configuration-dependent splitting from few meV up to hundredths of meV. This behaviour leads to a non-trivial dependence of the valley energetics. Configurations with well-separated conduction bands support additional optically active transitions beyond the conventional A and B excitons in MoS$_2$ and WS$_2$ monolayers, whereas nearly degenerate cases exhibit a reduced number of allowed transitions, observed for specific configurations at $x = 1/3$ and $x = 2/3$. These results demonstrate that the number and character of optically active transitions are governed not only by composition, but also by the microscopic arrangement of atoms. Moreover, we found the hole effective masses at the VBM show configuration-dependent anisotropy, reflecting sensitivity to local symmetry breaking and implying direction-dependent transport.

</details>


### [74] [Ultra-high-vacuum cluster tool for epitaxial synthesis and optical spectroscopy of reactive 2D materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14443)
*M. Dembecki,J. Schabesberger,M. Bissolo,A. Thurn,A. Ulhe,P. Avdienko,J. Ulrichs,H. Riedl,G. Koblmüller,E. Zallo,J. J. Finley*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 开发了用于不稳定二维材料生长和原位光学表征的全超高真空系统，结合分子束外延生长和低温光谱技术，实现了材料本征性质的无损分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要开发能够保持材料原始状态的生长和表征技术，以揭示不稳定二维半导体的本征性质，这对于半导体技术中的新型材料集成至关重要。

Method: 构建了全超高真空簇系统，结合分子束外延生长和低温（20K）光致发光/拉曼光谱技术；系统具有微米级空间分辨率和全晶圆扫描能力；开发了基于解卷积的算法，使用系统特定点扩散函数恢复振动下的空间信息。

Result: 系统在空间分辨率、20-300K温度控制（使用闭循环低温恒温器）和长期保存方面表现出色；成功应用于后过渡金属单硫族化合物的原位分析；算法有效恢复了振动条件下的空间信息。

Conclusion: 该系统实现了不稳定二维材料在原始状态下的原位结构和光电性质分析，为基础研究和可扩展二维材料生长优化提供了丰富且可重复的反馈，推动了先进器件集成的发展。

Abstract: The large-area synthesis of high-crystalline-quality two-dimensional (2D) materials is at the core of novel material integration for semiconductor technology. This effort relies on developing fabrication and characterization techniques that can uncover the material's intrinsic properties by preserving its pristine conditions. In this article, we present an all ultra-high-vacuum cluster for the growth using molecular beam epitaxy of 2D semiconductors that are unstable under ambient conditions and optical spectroscopy using low temperature (20 K) photoluminescence and Raman scattering. The optical chamber of the setup provides micrometer scale spatial resolution and the ability to scan the entire wafer. The performance of its setup regarding spatial resolution, temperature control over a temperature range of 20-300 K using a closed-cycle cryostat and long-term preservation are demonstrated using as-grown post-transition metal monochalcogenides. Furthermore, we introduce a deconvolution-based algorithm to recover spatial information under vibration using a system-specific point-spread function. This enables in situ analysis of the structural and optoelectronic properties of as-grown materials in their pristine form, providing rich and reproducible feedback for both fundamental studies and the optimization of scalable 2D material growth toward integration in advanced devices.

</details>


### [75] [Environment-dependent tight-binding models from ab initio pseudo-atomic orbital Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14470)
*Marco Buongiorno Nardelli*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 开发了一种环境依赖紧束缚框架，通过键屏蔽函数增强Slater-Koster跳跃积分，从伪原子轨道哈密顿量中提取参数，实现大体系从头算精度模拟


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统紧束缚模型参数固定，无法准确描述不同原子构型下的电子结构变化。需要发展能够捕捉局部配位环境影响的参数化方法，实现大尺度体系的高精度电子结构计算

Method: 基于伪原子轨道哈密顿量与Slater-Koster紧束缚模型的对应关系，开发环境依赖紧束缚框架。在Slater-Koster跳跃积分中加入键屏蔽函数来捕捉局部配位环境。通过同时拟合多个原子构型的PAO本征值谱来确定所有参数，打破屏蔽参数与跳跃参数之间的简并性

Result: 方法在四个原型系统上验证：块体铂、硅表面、Si/Ge[001]超晶格和扭曲双层石墨烯（最多4324个原子）。实现了从头算精度的大尺度电子结构计算，并与paoflow代码无缝集成

Conclusion: 开发的环境依赖紧束缚框架能够生成具有物理意义、可迁移的模型，可用于大尺度体系的电子、光学和输运性质计算，实现了从头算精度与计算效率的良好平衡

Abstract: \textit{Ab initio} pseudo-atomic orbital (PAO) Hamiltonians express the electronic structure of a solid in a compact, localized basis that spans the same Hilbert space as a conventional Slater--Koster tight-binding model, thereby providing an exact \textit{ab initio} representation without any loss of accuracy. Building on this correspondence, we develop an environment-dependent tight-binding (EDTB) framework in which Slater--Koster hopping integrals are augmented with bond-screening functions that capture the local coordination environment. All parameters are determined by fitting to the PAO eigenvalue spectrum across multiple atomic configurations simultaneously, which breaks the degeneracy between screening and hopping parameters and yields physically meaningful, transferable models capable of generating Hamiltonians for large systems with \textit{ab initio} precision. We demonstrate the efficiency and accuracy of the approach on four prototypical systems: bulk platinum, silicon surfaces, Si/Ge~[001] superlattices, and twisted bilayer graphene with up to $4{,}324$ atoms. The method is implemented in the \paoflow{} code and integrates seamlessly with its full post-processing suite, enabling the evaluation of a broad range of electronic, optical, and transport properties.

</details>


### [76] [An Investigation in the Kinetic Persistence of TiO$_2$ Polymorphs using Machine Learning Driven Pathfinding in Crystal Configuration Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14509)
*Max C. Gallant,David Mrdjenovich,Kristin A. Persson*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 开发新方法评估材料动力学稳定性，通过识别无扩散相变路径分析亚稳态多晶型体与基态相之间的势能景观


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着理论预测材料数量增加，需要评估材料在真实合成条件下的动力学可行性，而不仅仅是热力学稳定性。研究亚稳态多晶型体的动力学持久性与其和低能相之间势能景观地形的关系。

Method: 开发基于晶体标准形式的新方法，用于识别亚稳态多晶型体与基态对应相之间的无扩散相变路径。该方法利用晶体标准形式提供的晶体构型空间图表示，作为路径寻找算法的基板。

Result: 将方法应用于二氧化钛系统，该系统包含已知的锐钛矿、金红石和板钛矿相，以及多个假设的亚稳态多晶型体。分析这些路径的能量学与各相实验观察之间的关系。

Conclusion: 提出了一种评估材料动力学稳定性的新框架，通过分析相变路径的能量景观来预测亚稳态相的实验可观测性，为材料合成可行性提供了新的评估工具。

Abstract: As the number of theoretically predicted materials continues to grow, it becomes increasingly important to assess not only their thermodynamic stability but also their kinetic viability under realistic synthesis conditions. In this study, we investigate the hypothesis that the kinetic persistence of a metastable polymorph is related to the topography of the potential energy landscape separating it from lower energy phases. To accomplish this, we develop a new method for identifying diffusionless transformation pathways between metastable polymorphs and their ground-state counterparts and discuss the energetics of those pathways with respect to the experimental observation of each phase. This algorithm is underpinned by the recently developed Crystal Normal Form, which provides a graph representation of crystal configuration space and supplies the substrate for our pathfinding algorithm. We apply this method to the titanium dioxide system which contains the well-known anatase, rutile, and brookite phases in addition to a number of hypothetical metastable polymorphs.

</details>


### [77] [First-principles study of infrared, Raman, piezoelectric and elastic properties of Mg-IV-N\textsubscript{2} (IV = Ge, Si, Sn)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14554)
*Sarker Md. Sadman,Walter R. L. Lambrecht*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 对Mg-IV-N₂化合物（IV=Si, Ge, Sn）进行密度泛函微扰理论计算，分析其晶格振动模式、压电张量和弹性柔度张量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mg-IV-N₂化合物是超宽带隙半导体，具有多种潜在的电子和光电子应用价值。这些材料具有Pna2₁空间群结构，需要系统研究其振动特性以理解其光谱行为和力学性质。

Method: 采用密度泛函微扰理论（DFPT）计算Mg-IV-N₂化合物的振动模式，重点关注布里渊区中心点群对称性对应的振动模式，同时计算全布里渊区声子色散和态密度，并确定压电张量和弹性柔度张量。

Result: 建立了振动模式与其对应点群对称性之间的关系，这决定了材料的拉曼和红外光谱特性。获得了完整的布里渊区声子色散关系和态密度，并确定了压电张量和弹性柔度张量。

Conclusion: 通过DFPT计算全面表征了Mg-IV-N₂化合物的振动特性，为理解这些超宽带隙半导体的光谱行为、压电效应和弹性性质提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Mg-IV-N\textsubscript{2} compounds with IV=Si, Ge, Sn are ultra-wide band gap semiconductors with various potential electronic and optoelectronic applications. They share the \textit{Pna}2\textsubscript{1} space group crystal structure. Here we present Density Function Perturbation Theory (DFPT) calculations of the vibrational modes of these materials. We focus on the vibrational modes at the zone center to establish the relation between vibrational modes and their corresponding point-group symmetries, which determine the Raman and infrared spectra but also report the full Brillouin zone phonon dispersions and density of states. We also determine the piezoelectric tensor and the elastic compliance tensor.

</details>


### [78] [Anomalous Platinum and Oxygen Transport during Electroforming of NbOx Memristors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14680)
*Shimul Kanti Nath,Sanjoy Kumar Nandi,Xiao Sun,Sujan Kumar Das,Bin Gong,Nicholas J. Ekins-Daukes,Deepak Mishra,Mahesh P. Suryawanshi,William D. A. Rickard,Songyan Yin,Michael P. Nielsen,Robert G. Elliman*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究发现Pt/NbOx/Nb2O5/Pt忆阻器在电形成和操作过程中，不仅产生氧空位细丝，还导致铂电极金属的意外迁移，形成氧富集和铂富集共存的细丝结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为金属-氧化物-金属忆阻器的电形成仅涉及氧化物中氧空位细丝的形成，贵金属电极（如Pt、Au）保持化学惰性。本研究旨在探究Pt/NbOx/Nb2O5/Pt器件中电形成和操作的完整物理化学过程。

Method: 使用飞行时间二次离子质谱（ToF-SIMS）分析器件截面，揭示氧和铂的分布；结合有限元模型和集总元件模型，模拟电流控制负微分电阻操作产生的局部焦耳热和高频热循环效应。

Result: 发现微米尺度的氧富集细丝从Nb2O5层延伸穿过NbOx并深入Pt顶电极，同时沿同一路径形成铂富集细丝。模拟表明负微分电阻操作产生的局部加热和热循环强烈增强氧迁移，并促进铂沿空位富集路径的热辅助扩散。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了NbOx忆阻器中先前未被认识的金属离子传输机制，强调了电形成后电学动态在决定细丝化学、稳定性和器件可靠性中的关键作用，挑战了贵金属电极完全惰性的传统假设。

Abstract: Electroforming of metal-oxide-metal memristors is generally attributed to the creation of oxygen-vacancy filaments within the oxide, with noble metal electrodes such as Pt and Au remaining chemically inert. Here, we demonstrate that electroforming and subsequent operation of Pt/NbOx/Nb2O5/Pt devices can induce an unexpected and highly correlated redistribution of both oxygen and platinum. Time-of-flight secondary ion mass spectrometry reveals a filamentary pathway characterized by micrometer-scale oxygen enrichment extending from the Nb2O5 layer through NbOx and deep into the Pt top electrode. Surprisingly, this is accompanied by the formation of a Pt-rich filament penetrating the oxide stack along the same filamentary path. Finite-element and lumped-element modelling show that current-controlled negative-differential-resistance operation produces localized Joule heating and high-frequency thermal cycling, which strongly enhances oxygen migration and enables thermally assisted Pt diffusion along vacancy-rich pathways. These findings reveal a previously unrecognized metal-ion transport mechanism in NbOx memristors and highlight the critical role of post-forming electrical dynamics in determining filament chemistry, stability, and device reliability.

</details>


### [79] [Morphological Transition: From Meanders to Mound Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14750)
*Marta A. Chabowska,Hristina Popova,Magdalena A. Załuska-Kotur*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过元胞自动机模型揭示了晶体表面从蜿蜒图案到三维丘状结构的形态演化机制，发现该转变由Ehrlich-Schwoebel势垒与台阶上吸附原子迁移率的竞争所控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在理解平坦和倾斜晶体表面上丘状结构形成的不同生长行为，特别是倾斜表面从蜿蜒图案到三维丘状结构的平滑过渡机制，探索不同生长条件下表面形态演化的统一框架。

Method: 采用倾斜元胞自动机建模框架，模拟不同生长条件下的形态演化，包括沉积通量、表面扩散速率、温度和倾斜角度的变化。使用高度-高度相关函数计算沿台阶和跨台阶的相关长度，并分析其标度行为。

Result: 在中等势垒强度和足够高的台阶扩散率条件下，系统表现出从丘状构型到规则台阶蜿蜒图案的可逆转变。模拟揭示了动力学势垒与质量输运之间的复杂相互作用，并提供了连接不同表面结构类别的连续途径。

Conclusion: 研究建立了描述不同晶体生长机制下图案演化的统一框架，揭示了Ehrlich-Schwoebel势垒与吸附原子迁移率竞争对表面形态转变的关键控制作用，为理解晶体表面结构演化提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Mound formation on flat and miscut crystal surfaces exhibits distinct growth behaviors. While mound structures are the predominant feature on flat surfaces, miscut surfaces display a smooth transition from meandered patterns to three-dimensional mounds, depending on both internal and external conditions. We investigate this morphological evolution-from meander-like surface patterns to faceted pyramidal structures-using a vicinal Cellular Automata modeling framework. The transition is shown to be governed by the competition between the Ehrlich-Schwoebel barrier and adatom mobility on terraces. Under moderate barrier strengths and sufficiently high terrace diffusivity, the system demonstrates a reversible transition from mounded configurations to regular step meandered patterns. This reveals a complex interplay between kinetic barriers and mass transport. Our simulations cover a wide range of growth conditions, including variations in deposition flux, surface diffusion rates, temperature, and miscut angle. By applying the height-height correlation function, we calculate the correlation lengths along and across the steps and analyze their scaling behavior. These results offer insight into the continuum pathways that connect distinct classes of surface structures and provide a unified framework for describing pattern evolution across different crystal growth regimes.

</details>


### [80] [Nonmagnetic-magnetic Transitions in Rutile RuO2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14764)
*Yue-Fei Hou,Jiajun Lu,Xinfeng Chen,Gui-Bin Liu,Ping Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 金红石RuO₂的磁性基态存在争议，实验观察到非磁性和交变磁性两种状态。通过密度泛函理论计算发现，RuO₂的磁性对关联效应敏感且具有应变依赖性，存在多种交变磁性相，并在适当应变下可在非磁性和磁性态之间转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金红石RuO₂的磁性基态存在矛盾：不同晶体样品中实验观察到非磁性或交变磁性(AM)基态。需要合理的理论解释来调和这一矛盾，理解4d电子关联材料RuO₂的磁性本质。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论(DFT)计算，研究体相RuO₂的关联敏感性和应变依赖性磁性。在哈伯德参数空间中识别不同自旋磁矩大小的交变磁性相，并分析应变（显著改变晶胞体积）对基态转变的影响。

Result: 1. 在RuO₂的哈伯德参数空间中识别出多个具有不同自旋磁矩大小的交变磁性相；2. 当施加显著改变晶胞体积的适当应变时，RuO₂的基态可在非磁性态（无自旋劈裂）和磁性态（能带结构中有自旋劈裂）之间发生转变。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了RuO₂的磁性对电子关联效应敏感且具有应变依赖性，这一发现不仅展示了4d电子关联材料RuO₂中引人入胜的物理现象，也保留了其在自旋电子学应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Rutile RuO$_2$ has attracted great interest recently, as its magnetic ground state remains controversial. Experimental studies have reported either nonmagnetic or altermagnetic (AM) ground states in different crystalline samples of RuO$_2$, highlighting the need for a reasonable explanation to resolve this contradiction. In this study, density functional theory calculations are performed to reveal the correlation-sensitive and strain-dependent magnetism of bulk RuO$_2$. On one hand, multiple AM phases with different magnitudes of the spin magnetic moment are identified in the Hubbard parameter space for RuO$_2$. On the other hand, when appropriate strains which significantly change the crystal cell volume are applied, the ground state of RuO$_2$ can undergo transitions between the nonmagnetic state with no spin splitting and the magnetic states with spin splitting in the band structure. These findings not only demonstrate intriguing physics in 4d-electron-correlated RuO$_2$, but also retain its potential for spintronic applications.

</details>

Method: 开发了包含横向波矢依赖自旋极化的隧穿自旋输运理论，并应用于KV₂Se₂O反铁磁材料。通过第一性原理输运计算验证理论预测，构建KV₂Se₂O/MgO/KV₂Se₂O磁性隧道结模型。

Result: 理论预测KV₂Se₂O可达到磁阻的极端极限。第一性原理计算证实，基于KV₂Se₂O电极和少层MgO隔离层的磁性隧道结在零偏压下磁阻率大于7.57×10⁷%，且对偏压和隔离层厚度具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究为下一代自旋电子器件提供了定量设计原则，确立了KV₂Se₂O/MgO/KV₂Se₂O作为室温超高密度非易失性存储器的领先候选材料体系。

Abstract: Altermagnet-based heterojunctions have demonstrated magnetoresistive effects in experiments, however, a predictive theoretical model for non-ferromagnetic structures has remained elusive. In this work, we develop a tunneling-based spin-transport theory that explicitly incorporates the transverse-wavevector ($\bf{k}_\|$)-dependent spin polarization of an altermagnet's transport channels, enabling the prediction of giant tunneling magnetoresistance (TMR). Based on the theory, we predict that the altermagnet KV$_2$Se$_2$O can reach the extreme limit of magnetoresistance. By performing first-principles transport calculations, we verify that magnetic tunnel junctions using the metallic KV$_2$Se$_2$O as the electrodes and few-layer MgO as the spacer exhibit zero-bias magnetoresistance larger than $7.57\times10^7$\%, which is robust against the bias and thickness of the spacer. Our research provides a quantitative design principle for next-generation spin-electronic devices and establishes KV$_2$Se$_2$O/MgO/KV$_2$Se$_2$O as a leading candidate material system for room-temperature ultra-high-density non-volatile memory.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用透明共晶合金进行原位薄样品实验，并结合针对同一合金校准的二维相场模拟，研究不同熔化速度Vm和预凝固层状微观结构间距λ下的熔化模式。

Result: 观察到异常丰富的熔化图案多样性，实验与模拟结果吻合良好。揭示了三种主要物理机制：(1)高速Vm下液体沿固-固界面的渗透；(2)低速Vm下主相指状物的增厚；(3)小λ值下的周期倍增不稳定性，并建立了相应的标度行为。

Conclusion: 该研究为共晶熔化（包括增材制造过程中的熔化/凝固循环）的进一步研究提供了理论基础，揭示了熔化速度、微观结构间距与图案形成之间的复杂关系。

Abstract: We present a study of the melting dynamics of a two-phase eutectic solid. In situ, thin-sample experiments using a transparent eutectic alloy and two-dimensional phase field simulations calibrated for the very same alloy are combined to assess pattern formation during directional melting in a temperature gradient. Depending on the melting velocity $V_m$ and the spacing $λ$ of the pre-solidified lamellar microstructure, an unexpectedly rich diversity of melting patterns is observed, with good agreement between experiments and simulations. We unravel the different physical mechanisms leading to this diversity, and establish the scaling behaviors of (i) the penetration of the liquid along the solid-solid interface at large $V_m$, (ii) the thickening of the primary-phase fingers at low $V_m$, and (iii) a period-doubling instability for small $λ$ values. Our study provides a fundamental basis for further investigations of eutectic melting, including additive manufacturing during which melting/solidification cycles take place.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究不同密度泛函方法（PBE、HSE06及色散修正）在预测C3N4材料结构电子性质方面的准确性，为这类有前景的可见光光催化剂提供可靠的计算方法基础。

Method: 采用系统密度泛函理论研究，比较标准梯度修正泛函(PBE)、长程杂化泛函(HSE06)及色散修正(D3)方法；利用最佳方法研究体相三重态激子性质(S0-T1能量差、自陷激子能量、光致发光发射能量)；考察纳米结构化(0D和2D)和掺杂效应。

Result: HSE06-D3方法结果与实验数据和更复杂的G0W0计算吻合良好；波纹结构对所有方法都能进一步稳定层状结构；获得了体相三重态激子相关能量参数；纳米结构化和掺杂显著影响材料原子和电子结构。

Conclusion: HSE06-D3是研究C3N4材料结构电子性质的可靠方法，为这类可见光光催化剂的激子性质、纳米结构化和掺杂改性研究提供了计算基础，对材料设计有指导意义。

Abstract: In this systematic density functional theory study, we compare a standard gradient corrected functional (PBE) with a long-range hybrid functional (HSE06), with and without correction for the dispersion forces, relatively to their ability to correctly reproduce structural and electronic properties of different bulk 3D C3N4 phases, encompassing diamond- and graphitic-like models. Corrugation is found to provide further stabilization to the layered structures with all methods. We observe that HSE06-D3 method provides results in good agreement with experimental data and with more sophisticated G0W0 calculations. Based on that, we exploited the method to investigate the nature of the bulk triplet excitons in these C3N4 structures to evaluate the S0-T1 energy difference, the selftrapping triplet exciton energy and the photoluminescence emission energy, since this is a promising vis-light photocatalyst. Nanostructuring (0D and 2D) is another relevant aspect of these materials in practical applications, therefore we have considered the effect of single or multilayer exfoliation or space confinement in nanoparticles. Finally, we also discuss how the introduction of extrinsic dopants (e.g. S atoms) in the nanostructures modifies the atomic and electronic structure.

</details>


### [84] [Propagation of laser-generated GHz surface acoustic wavepackets in FeRh/MgO(001) below and above the antiferromagnetic-ferromagnetic phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14845)
*Ia. A. Mogunov,A. Yu. Klokov,N. Yu. Frolov,A. I. Sharkov,A. V. Protasov,G. E. Zhezlyaev,D. I. Devyaterikov,V. I. Zverev,A. M. Kalashnikova*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 使用160 fs激光脉冲在Fe49Rh51/MgO(001)系统中激发表面声波，通过时间分辨Sagnac干涉仪检测，分析声波振幅、频谱、相速度、群速度及其面内各向异性，特别关注FeRh薄膜磁相变对声波色散关系的调制。

Result: FeRh合金的反铁磁-铁磁相变伴随弹性性质的突变，通过调节样品温度和激光能量可控制声波激发效率和色散特性。获得了声波关键参数及其各向异性，确定了色散关系主要由FeRh薄膜决定。

Conclusion: FeRh薄膜作为光声换能器和机械负载，通过磁相变可有效调制表面声波传播特性，为磁声耦合器件设计提供了重要实验依据，特别是色散关系各向异性对声子-磁振子相干相互作用具有关键影响。

Abstract: Magnetoacoustic devices that harness the strong coupling between acoustic waves and magnons have emerged as a promising platform for energy-efficient spintronics. Laser-generated pulsed surface acoustic waves (SAWs) are particularly attractive for such applications, offering broadband frequency content up to the gigahertz (GHz) range, remote excitation without lithographic patterning, and surface localization for efficient on-chip integration. In this work, we present a comprehensive experimental study of laser-generated SAW pulses in the Fe49Rh51/MgO(001) system. A thin film of the near-equiatomic FeRh alloy serves both as an opto-acoustic transducer and as a mechanical load that modulates SAW propagation. The antiferromagnetic to ferromagnetic phase transition in FeRh, occurring slightly above room temperature, is accompanied by abrupt changes in its elastic properties, enabling controlled modification of the SAW excitation efficiency and dispersion characteristics by tuning the sample temperature and laser fluence. Using 160 fs laser pulses for excitation and time-resolved Sagnac interferometry for detection, we evaluated key SAW parameters, including amplitude, spectral content, phase and group velocities, and their in-plane anisotropy. Particular emphasis is placed on the dispersion relation and its anisotropy, which govern the coherent interaction between phonons and magnons and are determined primarily by the FeRh film.

</details>


### [85] [Reversable phase transitions in ferroic two-dimensional Nb2O2I4 through optically excited coherent phonons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14894)
*Chuanlin Liu,Dan Liu,Jie Guan,Chao Lian*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过rt-TDDFT研究二维铁电材料Nb2O2I4的光致相变，发现定制激光脉冲可激活特定相干声子模式，实现面内极化反转并诱导新型铁电相，具有可控可逆性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索二维铁电材料中光控相变的可能性，研究如何通过激光脉冲精确操控材料的多铁性相态，为下一代电子存储应用寻找高性能候选材料

Result: 激光脉冲可激活Γ点的A1-1和A1-2声子模式实现面内极化反转；精细调控激光参数可激发Y点和Γ点的额外声子模式，诱导产生三种反铁电相和一种铁电相；这些光致相变具有可控可逆性

Conclusion: 二维Nb2O2I4材料通过光控可实现多种铁电相之间的可逆转变，展现出优异的相态调控能力，是下一代电子存储应用的理想候选材料

Abstract: We investigate optically induced phase transitions in the two-dimensional (2D) ferroelectric (FE) material Nb2O2I4 using real-time time-dependent density functional theory (rt-TDDFT). Our results demonstrate that tailored laser pulses can activate specific coherent phonon modes. Specifically, the anharmonic atomic distortions of the A1-1 and A1-2 modes at the Γ-point facilitate the reversal of in-plane polarization. By fine-tuning laser parameters, additional phonon modes at both the Y and Γ points are excited. The resulting nonequilibrium atomic dynamics enable the formation of previously unreported ferroic phases, including three antiferroelectric (AFE) phases and one ferrielectric (FiE) phase. Notably, these optically induced phases can be reverted to the initial FE state using appropriate techniques. This controllable reversibility among multiple ferroic phases positions 2D Nb2O2I4 as a highly promising candidate for next-generation electronic storage applications.

</details>

<details>

Conclusion: 首次使用实验室级简单光学技术观察到反铁磁畴及其可移动性质，为反铁磁体的未来基础和应用研究提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Altermagnets are a new class of magnets accompanying global time-reversal symmetry breaking (TRSB) without net magnetization. The TRSB results in formation of novel altermagnetic domains. Features of altermagnetic domains, in particular their responses to external stimuli, are essentially important but yet unexplored. Here, we report visualization of bulk altermagnetic domains in MnTe based on scanning magneto-optical Kerr-effect microscopy using telecom infrared wavelength. We found two distinct TRSB domains with large Kerr rotations that do not scale with its tiny bulk magnetization. We also revealed controllability and stability of domains against magnetic or thermal perturbations. Our first observation of altermagnetic domains using a laboratory-scale simple optical technique showing their movable nature provide firm bases for future fundamental and application studies of altermagnets.

</details>


### [87] [Towards Non-van der Waals 2D Topological Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14976)
*Mani Lokamani,Gustav Bihlmayer,Gregor Michalicek,Daniel Wortmann,Stefan Blügel,Rico Friedrich*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究探索了非范德华二维材料中自旋轨道耦合对电子结构的影响，发现SbTlO3具有显著的自旋轨道耦合诱导能带分裂和能带反转特征，通过Pb替代Tl可调控拓扑性质，为非范德华二维拓扑绝缘体的系统研究奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非范德华二维材料中自旋轨道耦合对电子结构的影响，特别是考虑到这类材料通常具有表面阳离子终止和重元素存在，可能产生显著的相对论效应，但此前尚未得到详细探索。

Method: 研究三种非范德华二维材料AgBiO3、NaBiO3和SbTlO3，通过理论计算分析自旋轨道耦合对其电子结构的影响，特别关注能带重整化、能带分裂和能带反转现象。通过Pb替代Tl形成SbPbO3，进一步调控能带结构并分析拓扑不变量和边缘态。

Result: AgBiO3和NaBiO3在包含相对论效应后能隙附近的能带重整化可忽略不计，而SbTlO3显示出大的自旋轨道耦合诱导分裂（229meV）和能带反转特征。通过Pb替代Tl形成的SbPbO3将能带反转特征调控至费米能级附近，拓扑不变量分析和边缘态研究确认了200meV能隙内的拓扑性质。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了非范德华二维材料中自旋轨道耦合的重要作用，特别是SbTlO3及其衍生物表现出拓扑绝缘体特征，为系统研究稳健的非范德华二维拓扑绝缘体奠定了基础。

Abstract: Non-van der Waals two-dimensional (2D) materials derived from strongly bonded non-layered crystals have recently emerged as a novel and rising platform for nanoscale research. While uncovering and tuning their (opto-)electronic, catalytic, and magnetic properties has been the focus of intense research, the impact of spin-orbit coupling (SOC) onto their electronic structure has not yet been explored in detail. Studying these effects is, however, particularly relevant due to their surface cation termination and the presence of heavy elements in several representative compounds. Here, we investigate the effect of SOC onto the electronic structure of 2D AgBiO3, NaBiO3, and SbTlO3. While the first two systems show negligible band renormalization upon inclusion of relativistic effects around the band gap, SbTlO3 showcases a large SOC induced splitting (229meV) for the lowest conduction bands associated with a band inversion. Substitution of Tl with Pb forming SbPbO3 brings the band-inverted feature to the Fermi level. Analysis of topological invariants and investigation of edge states of zig-zag and armchair ribbons within the 200meV gap confirms the topological nature of the band splitting. Our work thus establishes a foundation for the systematic study of robust non-van der Waals 2D topological insulators.

</details>


### [88] [Controllable highly oriented skyrmion track array in Fe3GaTe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15019)

Conclusion: 该研究开发了一种工程化大面积、高度取向斯格明子配置的策略，为下一代自旋电子学和信息技术应用提供了新的途径。矢量磁场操控技术为可控生成和调控斯格明子阵列提供了有效方法。

Abstract: Magnetic skyrmions are emerging as promising candidates for next-generation information technologies, while the realization of scalable skyrmion lattices with tailored configurations is essential for advancing fundamental skyrmion physics and developing future applications. Here we achieved the controllable generation and regulation of a large-area, highly oriented skyrmion track array (STA) in ferromagnetic Fe3GaTe2 using a vector magnetic field manipulation technique. The orientation and ordering of STA, along with the types and density of skyrmions, are precisely controlled by modulating parameters during the manipulation. The critical roles of in-plane magnetic fields and Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction in STA generation is further confirmed by micromagnetic simulation. Our findings develop a strategy for engineering large-area and highly-oriented skyrmion configurations, offering a new pathway for the future application of next-generation spintronic and information technologies.

### [89] [Disentangling the ferroelectric phases of epitaxial hafnia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15081)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铪基铁电材料因其CMOS兼容性和在亚10纳米厚度下保持极化的能力而备受关注。虽然铁电行为通常归因于极性正交相(OIII)，但外延薄膜中也报道了具有菱方对称性的第二极性相(R相)。由于标准薄膜表征技术探测时两者差异细微，R相的性质一直存在争议。鉴于铁电材料的功能特性主要由晶体对称性决定，解决这一争议至关重要。

Method: 采用同步辐射掠入射衍射技术对两种相的外延薄膜进行广泛的3D倒易空间扫描。通过直接比较两种相的温度依赖性和电学响应，结合详细的晶体学分析来区分它们。

Result: 研究明确证实正交相(OIII)和菱方相(R相)是两个不同的相。通过3D倒易空间测量揭示了它们在晶体结构上的显著差异，温度依赖性和电学响应的直接比较进一步支持了这一结论，并提供了关于它们关键特性的深入见解。

Conclusion: 该工作通过先进的同步辐射衍射技术解决了铪基铁电材料中长期存在的相争议，明确区分了正交相和菱方相。这一发现对于理解铪基铁电材料的结构-性能关系具有重要意义，为未来基于这些相的铁电器件设计提供了关键的结构基础。

Abstract: Since its discovery, ferroelectric hafnia has been extensively studied due to its CMOS-compatibility and ability to remain polarized at sub-10 nm thicknesses. The ferroelectric behaviour is generally attributed to a polar orthorhombic (OIII) phase. However, a second polar phase with rhombohedral symmetry (R-phase) has also been reported in epitaxial films. The nature of the R-phase remains disputed due to the subtle differences with the OIII-phase when probed by standard thin film characterisation techniques. Given the functional properties of ferroelectrics are crucially determined by the crystal symmetry, resolving this matter is imperative. In this work, we settle the controversy through extensive 3D reciprocal space surveys made possible via synchrotron-based grazing incidence diffraction from epitaxial films of both phases. These experiments, together with direct comparison of their temperature dependence and electrical responses, conclusively establish them as two distinct phases and provide insight into their key characteristics.

</details>


### [90] [Fully Atomic-Layer-Deposited Vertical Complementary FeRAM with Ultra-High 2Pr > 100 uC/cm2 and High Endurance > 1E10 cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15131)
*Renhao Xue,Ruizhan Yan,Mansun Chan,Xiwen Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出垂直互补FeRAM架构，通过上下堆叠的互补极化铁电层实现差分极化求和，显著提升有效电荷窗口，解决HfO2基FeRAM剩余极化受限问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HfO2基FeRAM的有限剩余极化是密度缩放和可靠性的主要障碍，传统材料和工艺优化只能带来渐进式改进。这一限制源于厚度受限的可切换极化和HfO2基铁电体的固有极化上限。

Method: 提出全ALD生长的垂直互补FeRAM架构，其中顶部和底部堆叠的FeRAM单元保持互补极化。采用互补极化写入-读取方案，将读出从单层极化响应转换为差分极化求和，从而在不增加单个层切换电场或面积开销的情况下放大有效电荷窗口。

Result: VCF实现超过100 μC/cm²的有效差分极化，在1e10次开关循环后仍保持90 μC/cm²以上，无电击穿。在85°C下保持时间超过1e4秒，在V/3方案下经过1e6次干扰脉冲后有效差分极化仍高于80 μC/cm²。在5×5无选择器交叉点阵列中验证了阵列级操作。

Conclusion: VCF的性能提升源于全ALD生长工艺、器件架构和操作方案的协同优化，实现了高密度、宽存储窗口和强可靠性，为可扩展的FeRAM集成提供了解决方案。

Abstract: A limited remanent polarization (Pr) in HfO2-based FeRAM remains a key obstacle to density scaling and reliability, while material and process optimizations offer only incremental improvements. This limitation fundamentally originates from the thickness-constrained switchable polarization and the intrinsic polarization ceiling of HfO2-based ferroelectrics. Here, we propose an all-ALD-grown vertical complementary FeRAM (VCF) architecture, in which the top and bottom stacked FeRAM cells maintain complementary polarization. This complementary dipole configuration converts the readout from a single-layer polarization response into a differential polarization summation, thereby amplifying the effective charge window without increasing the switching field of each individual layer or incurring area overhead. Viewed from top to bottom, an "up-down" polarization pair stores logic '1', whereas a "down-up" pair stores logic '0'. Using a complementary polarization write-read scheme, the VCF achieves an effective differential polarization above 100 uC/cm^2 and retains above 90 uC/cm^2 after 1e10 switching cycles without electrical breakdown. Robust retention (longer than 1e4 s at 85 degC) and strong disturb immunity are demonstrated, with an effective differential polarization above 80 uC/cm^2 under a V/3 scheme after 1e6 disturb pulses. Array-level operation is validated in a 5 x 5 selector-free crosspoint array. The performance enhancement of the VCF arises from the co-optimization of the all-ALD-grown process, device architecture, and operation scheme, enabling high density, a wide memory window, and strong reliability for scalable FeRAM integration.

</details>


### [91] [Lattice dynamics and complete polarization analysis of Raman-active modes in LaInO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15156)
*Jonas Rose,Hai Nguyen,Moritz Meißner,Zbigniew Galazka,Roland Gillen,Georg Hoffmann,Oliver Brandt,Manfred Ramsteiner,Markus R. Wagner,Hans Tornatzky*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 结合偏振角分辨拉曼光谱和密度泛函理论计算，对正交晶系LaInO₃的拉曼活性声子模式进行全面分析，识别并归属了大部分Γ点声子到D₂ₕ点群的不可约表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究正交晶系LaInO₃的拉曼活性声子模式，通过实验和理论相结合的方法，全面表征其晶格动力学特性，为理解该材料的物理性质提供基础。

Method: 采用偏振角分辨拉曼光谱技术，从多个晶面取向进行背散射测量，结合全对称性分析；使用多维超光谱拟合程序从散射强度的角度依赖性中提取相对拉曼张量元素；进行第一性原理计算获得声子色散、声子态密度和原子位移模式。

Result: 识别并归属了大部分拉曼活性Γ点声子到D₂ₕ点群的不可约表示；即使对于强烈重叠的模式，也能从角度依赖性中提取相对拉曼张量元素；第一性原理计算得到的声子频率与实验模式频率吻合良好。

Conclusion: 通过实验与理论相结合的方法，成功表征了正交晶系LaInO₃的拉曼活性声子模式，为理解该材料的晶格动力学和物理性质提供了全面基础。

Abstract: In this study, we present a comprehensive analysis of the Raman active phonon modes in orthorhombic LaInO$_3$ based on a combination of polarization-angle resolved Raman spectroscopy and density functional theory calculations. By using backscattering from multiple crystallographic surface orientations and employing a full symmetry analysis, we identify and assign most of the Raman-active $Γ$-point phonons to their irreducible representations of the D$_{\rm{2h}}$ point group. A multidimensional hyperspectral fitting procedure allows us to extract the relative Raman tensor elements from the angular dependence of the scattering intensities, even for strongly overlapping modes. First-principles calculations yield the phonon dispersion along high-symmetry directions, the phonon densities of states, and atomic displacement patterns, which are found to be in good agreement with the experimental mode frequencies.

</details>


### [92] [Optimal spin-qubit hallmarks of sulfur-vacancy defects in 4H-SiC: Design from first principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15175)
*Marisol Alcántara Ortigoza,Sergey Stolbov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种4H-SiC中的缺陷结构VSiSC（硅空位+硫取代碳），具有自旋三重态基态，可作为光学控制的自旋量子比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找在宽带隙半导体中具有优异性能的自旋量子比特材料，特别是需要具有稳定的自旋三重态基态、光学可寻址性以及长自旋相干时间的缺陷结构。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算方法，包括GW近似和Bethe-Salpeter方程计算，分析VSiSC缺陷的电子结构、稳定性、光学性质以及自旋量子比特功能特性。

Result: 所有VSiSC构型都具有动态和热力学稳定的三重态基态，在带隙内形成尖锐孤立的电子态峰，在近红外光谱范围内具有强烈的光学激发，且宿主元素和掺杂剂具有零核自旋的高丰度同位素。

Conclusion: VSiSC缺陷是一种优异的光学控制自旋量子比特候选材料，具有稳定的自旋三重态基态、良好的光学可寻址性和潜在的长自旋相干时间。

Abstract: By applying our methodology, we propose a defect in 4H-SiC which combines a Si vacancy and a C atom substituted with S (VSiSC) to have a spin-triplet ground state with the spin qubit functionality. Our calculations confirm that all configurations of the defect have a dynamically and thermodynamically stable triplet ground state and higher energy singlet states, essential for the spin-qubit polarization cycle. From GW calculations, we found that the electronic states associated with the defect form sharp and isolated peaks within the band gap for both triplet and singlet states. Further Bethe-Salpeter-equation calculations show that all considered configurations have intense optical excitations in the near infrared spectrum range. Analysis of the excitation energies and rates indicate that the VSiSC defect can be an excellent optically controlled spin qubit. Crucially, the host elements and the dopant have high-abundance isotopes with zero nuclear spin ensuring high spin-coherence time of the qubit.

</details>

### [93] [On the inverse scattering transform for the KdV equation with summable initial data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14412)
*Alexei Rybkin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文为KdV方程在正半轴上具有L¹和L²可积初始条件的柯西问题提供了逆散射构造，通过左反射系数和Hardy空间上的Hankel算子得到了解的迹型表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究KdV方程在正半轴上具有L¹和L²可积初始条件的柯西问题，扩展标准短程势能理论，为更一般的可积半线支撑初始数据提供严格的逆散射构造。

Method: 使用左反射系数和Hardy空间H²上的Hankel算子，通过紧支撑势能的逼近、远离原点处相关反射系数的一致收敛性以及所得Hankel算子的连续性性质来推导解的迹型表示。

Result: 得到了对应解的迹型表示，为超出标准短程设置的一类可积半线支撑初始数据提供了严格的逆散射构造。

Conclusion: 该工作成功地将逆散射方法扩展到更一般的可积半线支撑初始数据，为KdV方程的柯西问题提供了新的解析工具和表示方法。

Abstract: We consider the Cauchy problem for the Korteweg--de Vries equation with real initial data $q$ that is both $L^1$ and $L^2$ summable and supported on (0,\infty). Using the left reflection coefficient and Hankel operators on the Hardy space $H^2$, we derive a trace-type representation for the corresponding solution. The proof is based on approximation by compactly supported potentials, uniform convergence of the associated reflection coefficients away from the origin, and continuity properties of the resulting Hankel operators. This yields a rigorous inverse scattering construction for a class of summable half-line supported initial data beyond the standard short-range setting.

</details>


### [94] [The $n$-Point Function of $t$-Core Partitions and Topological Vertex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14542)
*Chenglang Yang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文利用拓扑顶点研究t-core分划的n点函数，引入q变形n点函数，证明了相关函数是拟模形式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究t-core分划的n点函数，将Bloch-Okounkov研究的普通整数分划n点函数推广到t-core分划情况

Method: 使用拓扑顶点作为主要工具，引入q变形的n点函数，该函数同时推广了普通整数分划的n点函数和t-core分划情况

Result: 给出了t-core分划n点函数用theta函数表示的闭式公式，证明了相应的相关函数是拟模形式

Conclusion: 通过拓扑顶点方法成功建立了t-core分划n点函数的理论框架，将拟模形式性质从普通分划推广到t-core分划

Abstract: In this paper, we study the $n$-point function of $t$-core partitions. The main tool is the topological vertex, originally developed to study the topological string theory for toric Calabi--Yau 3-folds. By virtue of the topological vertex, we introduce the $q$-deformed $n$-point function that generalizes both the ordinary $n$-point function of all integer partitions studied by Bloch--Okounkov and $t$-core partition case treated here. As a consequence, we provide a closed formula for the $n$-point function of $t$-core partitions in terms of theta functions, and prove that the corresponding correlation functions are quasimodular forms.

</details>


### [95] [An inversion formula for the 2-body interaction given the correlation functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14681)

Method: 使用截断关联函数的无穷阶展开方法，在无限体积极限下分析势能对相互作用部分的收敛性

Abstract: Given a classical gas described by the truncated correlation functions of all orders, we prove convergence of an expansion of the pair interaction part of the (unknown) potential in terms of the truncated correlation functions of all orders, at infinite volume.

</details>


### [96] [Hamiltonian Monodromy in a Tavis-Cummings System with an $A_2$ Singularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14835)
*Konstantinos Efstathiou,Gabriela Jocelyn Gutierrez-Guillen,Pavao Mardešić,Dominique Sugny*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 三自由度可积哈密顿系统的奇异拉格朗日纤维化研究，基于双自旋Tavis-Cummings模型，发现了新的拓扑结构


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三自由度可积哈密顿系统的奇异拉格朗日纤维化研究相对缺乏，现有成果主要集中在二自由度系统，需要探索更高维度的例子来理解其全局结构

Method: 从双自旋Tavis-Cummings模型导出一个三自由度系统，分析其奇异拉格朗日纤维化的拓扑结构，包括最退化奇异纤维的同胚类型、分岔图、纤维化全局拓扑以及哈密顿单值性计算

Result: 发现了物理模型中未观察到的拓扑结构：最退化奇异纤维同胚于S²×S¹，具有A₂型奇异性；描述了分岔图和纤维化的全局拓扑，并计算了哈密顿单值性

Conclusion: 该研究为三自由度可积哈密顿系统的奇异拉格朗日纤维化提供了新案例，揭示了不同于现有物理模型的拓扑结构，拓展了对高维可积系统几何特性的理解

Abstract: Singular Lagrangian fibrations arising from three-degree-of-freedom integrable Hamiltonian systems remain largely unexplored. While several results describe the global structure of large classes of systems with two degrees of freedom, only a few examples are understood in higher dimensions. We present a three-degree-of-freedom system derived from the two-spin Tavis-Cummings model whose singular Lagrangian fibration exhibits a topology that has not been observed in other physical models. We show that the most degenerate singular fiber is homeomorphic to $\mathbf{S}^2\times\mathbf{S}^1$ with a singularity of $A_2$ type. We further describe the bifurcation diagram and the global topology of the fibration, and we compute its Hamiltonian monodromy.

</details>


### [97] [Blocking of 2D bistable reaction-diffusion fronts by obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15246)
*J. -G. Caputo,G. Cruz-Pacheco,J. Gatlik,B. Sarels*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究二维双稳态反应扩散波前在几何障碍物中的阻塞现象，建立定量判据预测波前传播


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推导波前在空间异质性存在时的传播定量判据，理解几何障碍如何影响反应扩散波前传播

Method: 采用守恒律方法，结合反应项积分作为有效驱动力，利用精确一维行波解构建简化解析模型

Result: 获得波导连接角度θ的锥形区域中波前传播的显式条件（宽度w<4），模型能同时捕捉几何和非线性效应，与数值模拟吻合良好

Conclusion: 成功建立预测阻塞阈值的简化模型，可扩展到更复杂几何结构（如棋盘状障碍物），推导出控制波前传播的简单启发式规则

Abstract: We investigate numerically the blocking of two-dimensional bistable reaction diffusion fronts by geometric obstacles. Our goal is to derive quantitative criteria for front propagation in the presence of spatial heterogeneities. Using a conservation law approach, we show that the integral of the reaction term acts as an effective driving force for the front. Combining this insight with the exact one-dimensional traveling wave solution, we construct a reduced analytical model that predicts blocking thresholds. In particular, we obtain explicit conditions for front propagation in a waveguide connected to a conical region of angle theta, valid for widths w less than 4. The model captures the influence of both geometry and nonlinearity, and shows good agreement with numerical simulations. Finally, we extend the analysis to more complex geometries, including checkerboard-like obstacles, and derive simple heuristic rules governing front propagation. ~

</details>

### [98] [Coarse Graining Reveals a Fluctuation-theorem-like Asymmetry in Financial Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14962)
*Jian Gao,Lufeng Zhang,Ping Fang,Pu Ke,Jin Wu,Yue Liu,Haijun Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于涨落定理的金融市场价格不可逆性诊断方法，通过对称的止盈止损规则比较多头和空头持仓时间分布，发现对数比值在尾部呈现线性关系，定义了有效的市场温度参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是将物理学中的涨落定理概念应用于金融市场分析。涨落定理展示了微观对称性如何通过粗粒化转化为可观测的不可逆性，作者希望构建类似的基于对称性的诊断工具来分析金融市场的不可逆性特征。

Method: 研究方法采用对称的止盈和止损规则，对同一价格序列生成多头和空头交易组合，比较两者的持仓时间分布。通过分析对数比值函数，识别分布间的交叉行为。使用Bachelier首次通过基准模型作为对比，并引入短时相关性机制来解释观察到的方向依赖性不对称。

Result: 研究结果显示，在股票指数、个股和加密货币市场中，多头和空头持仓时间分布的对数比值呈现稳健的交叉模式：在短持续时间保持近似常数，在尾部呈现线性关系，表明存在指数型方向不对称性。尾部斜率定义了有效的市场温度参数，作为选定观测尺度上波动强度的操作性度量。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了类似涨落定理的金融市场价格不可逆性诊断方法，表明通过粗粒化可观测量的复杂系统中存在可量化的不对称性特征。短时相关性机制能够解释方向依赖性的不对称性，为理解金融市场微观结构与宏观不可逆性之间的关系提供了新视角。

Abstract: Fluctuation theorems show how coarse graining transforms microscopic symmetry into observable irreversibility. Here we ask whether an analogous symmetrybased diagnostic can be constructed for financial markets. At the microscopic level, each transaction pairs a buyer and a seller, whereas trading decisions are typically made from coarse-grained price histories. Using symmetric takeprofit and stop-loss rules, we compare the holding-time distributions of long and short trading ensembles generated from the same price series. Across equityindices, individual stocks and cryptocurrencies, the log-ratio of the two distributions shows a robust crossover. It remains nearly constant at short durations but becomes linear in the tail, implying an exponential directional asymmetry. The tail slope defines an effective market temperature, an operational measure of fluctuation intensity on the chosen observation scale. A Bachelier first-passage benchmark captures the exponential tails but not the asymmetry, because long and short positions share the same leading decay rate. By contrast, short-time correlations between overlapping positions provide a minimal mechanism for the asymmetry by generating direction-dependent subleading relaxation spectra in a coarse-grained Markov description. Together, these results establish a fluctuation-theorem-like diagnostic of irreversibility in financial markets and, more broadly, in complex systems accessible only through coarse-grained observables.

</details>


### [99] [Thermodynamic Geometry of Relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15000)
*Hao Wang,Li Zhao,Shuai Deng,Yu-Han Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出基于瑞利商的几何度量，将弛豫描述为熵刚度和摩擦耗散之间的竞争，以范德瓦尔斯气体为例展示弛豫景观，发现临界温度附近慢模弛豫率线性消失


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡态和驱动非平衡过程的几何描述已清晰，但弛豫向平衡过程的几何描述仍缺乏。本文旨在建立弛豫过程的几何框架，完善热力学几何理论体系。

Method: 提出基于瑞利商的热几何度量，将弛豫重新表述为熵刚度和摩擦耗散之间的基本竞争。以具有两个耗散通道的范德瓦尔斯气体为例，明确展示其弛豫景观。

Result: 发现接近临界温度Tc时，慢模弛豫率线性消失：λ_s ∝ (T-Tc)/Tc，表明临界减速现象。该几何度量成功描述了弛豫动力学特征。

Conclusion: 本研究完善了热力学几何框架，为表征复杂系统的弛豫动力学提供了通用工具，建立了弛豫过程的几何描述方法。

Abstract: While the geometry of equilibrium states and driven non-equilibrium processes is clearly understood, a geometric description for relaxation towards equilibrium is still lacking. Here, we propose a thermo-geometric measure based on the Rayleigh quotient, reformulating relaxation as a fundamental competition between entropic stiffness and frictional dissipation. Taking a van der Waals gas with two dissipation channels as an example, we explicitly demonstrate its relaxation landscape. Particularly, we find that upon approaching the critical temperature $T_c$, the slow-mode relaxation rate vanishes linearly as $λ_s \propto (T-T_c)/T_c$, indicating critical slowing down. This study completes the thermodynamic geometry framework, providing a general tool for characterizing the relaxation dynamics of complex systems.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Long-range spin-polarized Josephson effect in ballistic S/F/S junctions with precessing magnetization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14390)
*E. S. Andriyakhina,M. Mansouri,M. Breitkreiz,P. W. Brouwer*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个关于具有均匀进动磁化的弹道N/F/S和S/F/S结的理论，该进动产生长程等自旋超导关联，导致非平衡安德烈夫束缚态分布和强非正弦电流-相位关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有进动磁化的超导-铁磁结中的非平衡效应，探索通过磁化进动控制超导输运特性的可能性，特别是在完全极化半金属极限下实现开关行为。

Method: 建立弹道N/F/S和S/F/S结的理论模型，考虑均匀进动磁化产生的长程等自旋超导关联，分析非平衡安德烈夫束缚态分布，推导电流-相位关系，分别处理部分极化和完全极化铁磁体情况。

Result: 对于大进动角，电流-相位关系呈现强非正弦特性；在完全极化半金属极限下，磁化进动可将结从亚能隙电流为零的"关"状态切换到具有有限安德烈夫电导和有限约瑟夫森电流的"开"状态。

Conclusion: 磁化进动能显著改变超导-铁磁结的输运特性，特别是在完全极化铁磁体中可实现开关效应，为基于自旋动力学的超导器件设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We present a theory of ballistic N/F/S and S/F/S junctions with a uniformly precessing magnetization, which generates long-range equal-spin superconducting correlations [Takahashi et al., Phys. Rev. Lett. 99, 057003 (2007), Houzet, Phys. Rev. Lett. 101, 057009 (2008)]. The non-equilibrium distribution of Andreev bound states leads to a strongly non-sinusoidal current-phase relationship for large precession angles. We derive detailed results for ballistic junctions involving partially and fully polarized ferromagnets. In the fully polarized half-metal limit, the magnetization precession switches the junction from an "off" state with vanishing subgap current to an "on" state with finite Andreev conductance and finite Josephson current.

</details>


### [101] [Josephson phase shift and diode effect due to the inverse spin Hall effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14521)
*Gen Tatara,Yositake Takane,Aurelien Manchon*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究超导-正常金属-超导结中由空间反演不变的spin-orbit相互作用引起的直接和逆自旋霍尔效应


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在保持结构反演对称性的系统中实现自旋霍尔效应的新机制，不同于需要打破结构反演对称性的Rashba系统

Method: 理论分析超导-正常金属-超导结，考虑空间反演不变的自旋轨道相互作用，研究超电流诱导的自旋霍尔效应和空间非均匀静态磁场诱导的逆效应

Result: 超电流诱导静态自旋积累，在两端产生相反极化；空间非均匀静态磁场诱导异常相移，在存在高次谐波时产生二极管效应

Conclusion: 提出了一种不需要打破结构反演对称性的自旋霍尔效应机制，为自旋电子学在超导系统中的实现提供了新途径

Abstract: We theoretically study the direct and inverse spin Hall effects in a superconductor-normal metal-superconductor junction induced by a spin-orbit interaction that is invariant under spatial inversion. We show that a supercurrent induces a spin Hall effect, leading to a static spin accumulation with opposite polarizations at the two edges, analogous to that in normal conductors. For the inverse effect, we consider a spatially inhomogeneous static magnetic field and show that it induces an anomalous phase shift, which, in the presence of higher harmonics, results in a diode effect. Unlike Rashba systems, the present mechanism does not require broken structural inversion symmetry.

</details>


### [102] [Effect of Rashba spin-orbit coupling on Faraday rotation in an extended Haldane model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14665)
*Yuan Fang,Yixiang Wang,Xiaopu Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文研究了Rashba自旋轨道耦合对扩展Haldane模型中法拉第旋转谱的影响，发现Rashba SOC可调控FR峰值位置和强度，为磁光器件设计提供新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用拓扑材料的法拉第旋转特性开发新型磁光器件具有广阔前景。研究Rashba自旋轨道耦合对FR谱的影响，探索通过SOC工程优化器件性能的可能性。

Method: 在扩展Haldane模型中引入Rashba SOC和交换劈裂，采用Kubo公式计算FR谱，分析不同拓扑相（特别是陈数C=2区域）的FR特性，并通过低能有效哈密顿量验证紧束缚模型计算结果。

Result: 在C=2区域，无交换劈裂时FR角可超过4°，峰值位置可通过Rashba SOC调控；加入交换劈裂后，FR谱在宽频范围内呈现近乎平坦的轮廓，且FR峰值随Rashba SOC强度单调增加。Rashba SOC开启额外跃迁通道，其净贡献对FR峰值产生增强效应。

Conclusion: Rashba SOC工程可用于设计和优化磁光器件特性，扩展Haldane模型中的SOC调控为实现可调谐FR器件提供了理论依据和设计思路。

Abstract: Utilization of Faraday rotation (FR) properties of topological materials offers a promising route toward novel magneto-optical devices. We systematically investigated the effect of Rashba spin-orbit coupling (SOC) on FR spectra in an extended Haldane model, which incorporates Rashba SOC and exchange splitting into the original spinless Haldane framework. Using the Kubo formalism, we calculated the FR spectra across the model's rich topological phase diagram. We found that in the Chern number C=2 region, in the absence of exchange splitting, the FR angle can exceed 4$^\circ$ and its peak position is tunable by the Rashba SOC. In contrast, with the inclusion of exchange splitting, a nearly flat FR profile emerges over a broad frequency range, and the FR peak values increase monotonically with the Rashba SOC strength. The Rashba SOC opens additional transition channels, whose net contribution constructively enhances the FR peak. Furthermore, we derived a low-energy effective Hamiltonian expanded up to quadratic terms, the results of which are in good agreement with tight-binding model calculations, thereby validating our numerical results. Our findings suggest that magneto-optical device characteristics can be designed and optimized through Rashba SOC engineering.

</details>


### [103] [Orbitals of Artificial Atoms in a Gapped Two-Dimensional Vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14737)
*Mong-Wen Gu,Aizhan Sabitova,Taner Esat,Christian Wagner,F. Stefan Tautz,Aleksandr Rodin,Ruslan Temirov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究利用扫描隧道显微镜可视化二维分子薄膜中纳米结构的人工原子束缚态，发现其电子态既模拟真实原子的s和p轨道，又因二维带隙真空环境产生全新的准一维局域态，扩展了化学轨道词汇表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米技术能够制造模拟真实原子电子态的人工原子，理解这些人工原子的相互作用和成键机制对于设计具有定制电子特性的新材料至关重要。研究旨在探索人工原子在二维带隙真空环境中的独特电子态行为。

Method: 使用扫描隧道显微镜（STM）对二维分子薄膜中图案化的纳米结构进行可视化研究。该薄膜具有抛物线能带和多个部分能隙，通过STM直接观测人工原子的束缚态电子分布。

Result: 观察到最低能态从能带底部分离，其电子分布与真实原子的s和p轨道相似，成键方式也相同。更重要的是，人工原子所处的二维带隙真空环境产生了真实原子中没有的全新轨道类型——准一维局域态，这些轨道主要由周围电子真空环境塑造。

Conclusion: 人工原子不仅能够模拟真实原子的电子行为，还能在二维带隙真空环境中产生全新的准一维局域轨道，这丰富了化学的轨道词汇表，为设计具有定制电子特性的新材料提供了新途径。

Abstract: Advances in nanotechnology now allow the creation of artificial atoms - engineered structures whose electronic states closely mimic those of real atoms. Understanding how these artificial atoms interact and bond is key to designing new materials with tailored electronic properties. Here, we use scanning tunnelling microscopy to visualise the bound states of nanostructures patterned in a two-dimensional molecular film featuring a parabolic band with multiple partial energy gaps. The lowest-energy states split off from the bottom of the band and resemble the familiar $s$ and $p$ orbitals of natural atoms, even bonding in the same way. Yet, artificial atoms go beyond this analogy: the gapped two-dimensional vacuum in which they reside gives rise to entirely new orbitals with no counterparts in real atoms. These quasi-one-dimensional localised states enrich the orbital vocabulary of chemistry, adding a new class of orbitals that are predominantly shaped by the surrounding electronic vacuum.

</details>


### [104] [Thermal conductivity tuning of scalable nanopatterned silicon membranes measured with a three-probe method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14770)
*Jose M. Sojo-Gordillo,Alex Rodriguez-Iglesias,Dominik M. Koch,Arianna Nigro,Iñigo Martin-Fernandez,Marta Fernandez-Regulez,Marc Salleras,Ilaria Zardo*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用嵌段共聚物自组装制备纳米孔硅膜，通过改进三探针技术实现热导率精确测量，证明纳米孔蚀刻深度可有效调控热输运，实现室温热导率五倍降低


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声子硅结构作为可集成、可扩展的纳米系统在调控热输运方面具有潜力，但其复杂制造工艺限制了广泛应用，同时悬浮纳米结构薄膜的热性能可靠表征因热接触电阻问题而具有挑战性

Method: 采用嵌段共聚物自组装方法制备周期63nm、孔径35nm的纳米孔硅膜；开发改进的三探针技术，能够对复杂薄膜系统进行鲁棒、定量、空间分辨的热导率测量，并考虑热接触伪影

Result: 在30-350K温度范围内验证了未图案化40nm厚硅薄膜的热导率测量，室温热导率为46.5W/m·K；纳米孔蚀刻深度控制可有效调控热输运，完全蚀穿膜在室温下实现热导率五倍降低至7.3W/m·K

Conclusion: 纳米孔蚀刻深度控制是声子硅中调控热输运的有效参数，嵌段共聚物自组装结合改进三探针技术为纳米结构薄膜的热性能精确表征和调控提供了可行途径

Abstract: Phononic silicon structures have emerged as an integrable and scalable nanosystem for tailoring thermal transport. However, their widespread adoption has been limited by their complex fabrication pathways. Alongside, the reliable characterization of thermal properties in suspended nanostructured films remains challenging, as thermal contact resistances often hinder the accuracy of measurements.
  In this work, we demonstrate a clear and controllable reduction of thermal conductivity in nanopatterned silicon membranes. A block copolymer self-assembly approach is employed to fabricate nanoholed silicon films with a pitch of 63 nm and hole diameters of 35 nm. Additionally, we introduce an extension of the three-probe technique that enables robust, quantitative, and spatially resolved thermal conductivity measurements in complex thin-film systems, accounting for thermal contact artifacts.
  The method is validated through measurements on unpatterned 40 nm-thick silicon thin films between 30 and 350 K, yielding a room-temperature thermal conductivity of 46.5 W/m.K. Finally, we further show that controlled etching of the nanoholes provides a powerful means to tune thermal transport in the overall studied temperature range, establishing hole etch depth control as an effective parameter in phononic silicon. Specifically, a fivefold reduction in thermal conductivity is achieved, reaching 7.3 W/m.K for fully etched-through membranes at room temperature.

</details>


### [105] [Formalizing Poisson-Boltzmann Theory for Field-Tunable Nanofluidic Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14777)
*Zhongyuan Zhao,Chudi Qi,Yuheng Li,Shoushan Fan,Qunqing Li,Yang Wei*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究通过形式化重构Poisson-Boltzmann理论，建立了可调谐纳米流体传输的正式框架，揭示了EDL的不同区域，并预测了静电调制的两个基本热力学极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米流体器件因其受限电双层对多种外部物理场的敏感性而展现出非常规离子传输特性，在能源和信息技术中具有应用前景。虽然实验研究进展迅速，但对场可调谐纳米流体传输的理论理解尚未达到形式化和统一化的水平。

Method: 形式化重构Poisson-Boltzmann理论，在参数空间上揭示不同的电双层区域，并基于区域分类建立可调谐纳米流体传输的正式理论框架。

Result: 该框架能够重现观察到的电导率-浓度标度行为，合理解释具有可重构极性的离子晶体管，并预测静电调制的两个基本热力学极限（60 mV/dec和120 mV/dec）。

Conclusion: 该框架具有准确性、普适性和可扩展性，能够解释受限空间中广泛的离子传输现象，为纳米流体器件的理性设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Nanofluidic devices prompts unconventional ion transports appealing to energy and information technologies, thanks to the susceptibility of confined electric double layers (EDL) to various external physical fields. Although experimental studies advance rapidly, the rationalization of field-tunable nanofluidic transports has not reached a formalized and unified level. Here we formally reformulate the Poisson-Boltzmann theory and reveal distinct EDL regimes on the parameter space. Based on the regime classification, we establish a formal framework for the tunable nanofluidic transport, which reproduces the observed conductivity-concentration scaling behaviors, rationalizes the ionic transistors with reconfigurable polarities, and predicts two fundamental thermodynamic limits for electrostatic modulation (60 mV/dec and 120 mV/dec). Being accurate, generalizable and extensible, this framework can account for a wide range of ion transports in confined spaces.

</details>


### [106] [Weak Magnetic Sensing via Floquet Driving in an Active Cavity Magnon Coupled System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14780)
*Fan Yang,Xudong Wang,Lijun Yan,Yue Zhao,Jinwei Rao,Lihui Bai,Shishen Yan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 基于PCB实现的微波腔-YIG耦合系统，通过电调增益补偿损耗，利用Floquet调制检测交变弱磁场，室温下达到121 pT/√Hz灵敏度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高灵敏度弱磁场检测技术通常需要低温操作或庞大设备，限制了实际应用。本文旨在开发一种室温工作、小型化的高灵敏度交变磁场传感器。

Method: 采用印刷电路板实现有源微波腔与钇铁石榴石耦合系统，通过电调增益补偿腔体损耗以提高品质因子和信号强度。利用交变磁场诱导Floquet调制，通过驱动特定混合模式并测量产生的Floquet边带来实现弱场检测。

Result: 该微型化器件在室温下工作，实现了121 pT/√Hz的检测极限，显著优于传统方法在小型化、室温操作方面的限制。

Conclusion: 基于PCB的有源微波腔-YIG耦合系统为室温弱磁场检测提供了一种有效的小型化解决方案，通过增益补偿和Floquet调制实现了高灵敏度检测。

Abstract: While significant advancements have been made in weak magnetic field detection, conventional high-sensitivity techniques are often limited by requirements for cryogenic operation or bulky setups. In this work, we develop a sensitive alternating magnetic field sensor based on a coupled system of an active microwave cavity and yttrium iron garnet (YIG), with the components implemented on printed circuit boards (PCBs). By introducing electrically tunable gain to compensate for cavity losses, we substantially improve both the quality factor and the signal intensity. Under the coupled system, Floquet modulation is induced by the alternating magnetic field, allowing for weak field detection by driving a specific hybrid mode and measuring the resulting Floquet sidebands. This miniaturized device operates at room temperature, achieving a detection limit of 121 pT/\sqrt{Hz}.

</details>


### [107] [Layer-dependent quantum transport in KV2Se2O-based altermagnetic tunnel junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14817)
*Yue Zhao,Bin Xiao,Jiawei Liu,Hui Zeng,Jun Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究设计了一种基于KV₂Se₂O/SrTiO₃/KV₂Se₂O结构的交变磁隧道结，通过第一性原理计算揭示了界面层数奇偶性对量子输运的调控机制，预测了高达4.6×10⁷%的巨大隧道磁阻效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统磁隧道结的微型化受到杂散磁场的限制，而交变磁体结合了铁磁体和反铁磁体的优势，具有无杂散磁场、巨大自旋劈裂和高本征频率等特性，为开发高性能磁隧道结提供了新途径。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论结合非平衡格林函数方法，研究基于KV₂Se₂O/SrTiO₃/KV₂Se₂O结构的交变磁隧道结的层数依赖量子输运特性和隧道磁阻效应。

Result: 传输特性随SrTiO₃层数呈现显著振荡行为：奇数层时O-Se界面具有平滑有效势能，支持横向动量输运通道，传输增强；偶数层时Ti-Se界面势能更陡峭，抑制横向动量输运。使用4层SrTiO₃时可实现4.6×10⁷%的巨大隧道磁阻。

Conclusion: 研究不仅深化了对交变磁隧道结中量子输运的物理理解，还揭示了势垒界面工程是调控磁电性能的有效策略，为设计高性能自旋电子器件提供了新思路。

Abstract: Magnetic tunnel junction (MTJ) is the key component to enable information access and increasing number of MTJs is integrated to develop high-density spintronic devices. However, continuous miniaturization of the conventional MTJs is hindered by stray magnetic fields. Altermagnets, combining the advantages of both ferromagnets and antiferromagnets, provide a promising alternative to fabricate versatile MTJs with exotic properties, such as giant spin splitting, high intrinsic frequency, and absence of stray fields. Inspired by the altermagnetic metal candidate KV2Se2O reported recently, we design an altermagnetic tunnel junction (AMTJ) based on KV2Se2O/SrTiO3/KV2Se2O. Using density functional theory combined with non-equilibrium Green's function, we investigate the layer-dependent quantum transport properties and the tunneling magnetoresistance (TMR) of such AMTJ device. Our calculated results reveal that the transmission of the AMTJ device exhibits a pronounced oscillation behavior dependent on the number of layers of the SrTiO3 semiconductor, which is attributed to the interface configuration determined by parity of the layer number. In odd-layer devices, the electron-rich O-Se interface exhibits a smooth effective potential and enables transverse momentum (k||) transport channels, leading to enhanced transmission. In contrast, in even-layer devices, the Ti-Se interface presents a steeper effective potential, impeding quantum transport through transverse momentum (k||) channels. A giant TMR of 4.6*10^7% is predicted to be realized by using a 4-layer SrTiO3. Our findings not only provide physical understanding relevant to the quantum transport in AMTJs, but also unveil that the barrier interface engineering is a strategy to tune the magnetoelectric performance.

</details>


### [108] [Poor man's Majorana bound states in quantum dot based Kitaev chain coupled to a photonic cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15036)
*Francesco Buonemani,Alvaro Gómez-León,Marco Schirò,Olesia Dmytruk*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 量子点平台通过光子腔调控实现Kitaev链中的poor man's Majorana束缚态，腔场可筛选粒子相互作用达到甜点条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子点平台为实现Kitaev链哈密顿量和Majorana束缚态提供了有前景的途径，但需要精细调控参数达到甜点条件。研究探索如何通过光子腔嵌入来工程化poor man's Majorana束缚态。

Method: 基于先前工作，构建基于量子点与邻近效应的微观模型，并将其嵌入光子腔中。分析光子耦合对有效哈密顿量的影响，特别是对配对项的调控作用。

Result: 光子耦合产生的有效哈密顿量中，腔场影响配对项。腔场可筛选粒子相互作用：零光子态可抵消吸引相互作用，单光子态可屏蔽排斥相互作用。大量光子时跳跃振幅被抑制，导致简并谱。

Conclusion: 量子光可用于工程化poor man's Majorana束缚态，通过腔场嵌入调控相互作用达到甜点条件，为实现Majorana束缚态提供了新途径。

Abstract: Quantum dot based platforms offer a promising route towards realizing the Kitaev chain Hamiltonian hosting Majorana bound states (MBSs). Poor man's MBSs arise in a two-site Kitaev chain when the parameters of the system are fine-tuned to the sweet spot. Based on our previous work [Phys. Rev. B 111, 155410 (2025)], we consider a microscopic model for the Kitaev chain based on quantum dots with proximity effect embedded in a photonic cavity. We find that the photon coupling in the microscopic model yields an effective Hamiltonian where the cavity affects the pairing term. However, we demonstrate that even in this case, it is possible to screen particle interactions and reach the sweet spot condition for the emergence of the poor man's MBSs. In particular, we find that attractive particle interactions can be canceled for the cavity prepared in the zero-photon state, while repulsive ones can be screened with a cavity prepared in the one-photon state. Furthermore, in case of a large number of photons in the cavity, we find that the hopping amplitudes are suppressed resulting in a degenerate spectrum. This motivates the use of quantum light for engineering poor man's MBSs with cavity embedding.

</details>


### [109] [New frontiers in quantum science and technology using van der Waals Josephson junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15276)
*Joydip Sarkar,Ayshi Mukherjee,Amit Basu,Ritajit Kundu,Arijit Kundu,Mandar M. Deshmukh*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 这篇综述回顾了基于范德华材料的约瑟夫森器件在过去十年的快速发展，探讨了其在超导量子技术中的独特优势和潜在应用，同时指出了规模化挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统约瑟夫森结在制造上已达到工业水平，但范德华材料提供了探索新功能和相关物理的独特途径，这些在传统约瑟夫森结中无法实现。范德华材料的多样性、对称性控制和长弛豫时间为创造新型量子器件提供了机会。

Method: 通过综述分析的方式，整合了范德华约瑟夫森结的最新进展，包括材料多样性、先进制造技术、对称性控制、长弛豫时间特性，以及与扭曲电子学和拓扑物理的交叉融合。

Result: 范德华约瑟夫森结展现出独特的物理特性和功能，为超导量子技术开辟了新方向，包括量子计算和超灵敏混合传感器等应用。然而，规模化制造仍是需要克服的挑战。

Conclusion: 范德华约瑟夫森结代表了超导电子学领域的范式转变，通过与扭曲电子学和拓扑物理的结合，有望重新定义超导量子技术。该综述为研究人员提供了该领域的现状总结和发展路线图。

Abstract: Over the last decade, the development of Josephson devices based on van der Waals (vdW) materials has advanced rapidly, representing a paradigm shift driven by the advent of 2D materials. The diverse vdW materials library, combined with advanced fabrication techniques, enables the integration of materials with vastly disparate properties for scientific exploration. The vdW Josephson junctions (JJs) offer a unique route to explore novel functionalities and associated physics that remain inaccessible in conventional JJs, which have reached an industrial level in terms of fabrication. Beyond material diversity, vdW crystalline materials offer fundamental new control over device symmetries, enabling the realization of Hamiltonians unique to 2D systems. Furthermore, the long relaxation times of myriad excitations in 2D heterostructures open possibilities for creating exquisite quantum sensors, with the 2D material itself acting as an efficient bus for transmitting excitations to the active sensing element. This creative explosion in vdW-based superconducting electronics is rapidly growing, and our review highlights the resulting devices and physics. The confluence of vdW JJs with twistronics and topology has the potential to redefine superconducting quantum technology, enabling applications from quantum computation to ultra-sensitive hybrid sensors. While opportunities abound with vdW JJs, the challenge of scalability must be surmounted for translation into real-world devices. This review synthesizes current developments and offers a roadmap for researchers navigating this burgeoning field.

</details>
